- **2026-08-16 (rev 123)** — **The Price zone becomes the band and nothing else: a titled card with a chart of how services are priced, and the three preset rows come off.** The designer on the built `listings-filters`, with a Figma reference (`YlGWlsWWjKSCxhONMzGG2F`, node `94:6`): *«зроби такий вю … заголовок Діапазон цін, підзаголовок ціна послуги, і підпис внизу мінімум та максимум, такий барчарт з булітами»*. **The one thing the reference did not answer was put to her before anything was built** — it carries no `Low / Medium / High`, so either the presets stay above the chart or they go — and **both readings conform**, which is what she was told first: the native sliders already carry the arrow keys unscripted (`WCAG 2.1.1` ✓) so the bands were a shortcut and never the only path; thumbs are **24px** in a **44px** lane (`2.5.8` AA floor, `HIG · 44pt` ✓) either way; and the chosen band is printed in words either way (`1.4.1` ✓). She took **replace**, so the card is exactly the reference. **rev 66's open departure closes on her own instruction rather than being argued down.** That entry recorded `Low / Medium / High` as a `voice.md` **P2** breach taken on her word — *three adjectives, no checkable figure, on a screen whose cards carry no price at all* — and named the range's readout as the honest half of the zone. The adjectives are gone; the zone now states nothing but figures. **What goes with them is the `Insured` chip, and that is a loss, not a tidy-up:** `RJ-C1` is the highest single drop-off in the CJM and this was the filter sheet's only trust mark. rev 66 had already flagged it as readable *the cheaper two are not* — `.dr-chip--trust` sits on **every** operator card in the built product — so the removal mends that reading while costing the sheet its one credential. `.dr-chip--claim` is untouched and returns to any row on her word. **THE ANATOMY, MEASURED ON THE BUILD** (375 × 812, annotations off): card **341 × 254.98** on a 16px inset all round, against **341 × 258.50** for the three rows plus the old band — **the zone gained a title, a subtitle and a 40-bar chart and got 3.52px shorter**. Panel scroll height **856 → 853** in a **577** window; the zone still opens **682.56px** down, below the fold as it has been since rev 118, and that is unchanged rather than fixed. Title `--t-title` 17/600 `--ink` **14.37:1**; subtitle `--t-bodysm` 13/400 `--slate` **5.95:1**; captions `--t-meta` 12.5 `--slate` **5.95:1**; values `--t-heading` 15/600 `--ink` on a `--media` ground at `--r-input`, **13.33:1**. **The value keeps a ground for rev 118's reason and is the fourth field in the system to hold one** — after the search bar, the empty multiline area and the clock — *there is no label beside it to say it is a control's readout*. **The reference draws those two fields as full pills and this build gives them `--r-input` 10**: the ramp's own shape for a field, where `--r-pill` in this system belongs to chips. A departure from the reference, not from the ramp; one line if she wants the reference's shape. **THE CHART IS A PICTURE OF SUPPLY AND IS NEVER THE CARRIER OF THE CHOICE.** 40 bars at **5.77px** with a 2px gap across **309** — each bar is a **₴25** slice, so the slider's **₴50** step is exactly two bars and the shading can never land half a bar out of step. Verified by key rather than asserted: one `ArrowRight` from the floor takes `₴0 → ₴50` and the in-band count **40 → 38**; a thumb pushed past its partner clamps (`800` against a max of `500` gives `500 / 500`); `₴150 – ₴550` shades **16** bars and prints both figures. In-band `--ink` on `--card` **14.37:1**, out-of-band `--btn2` **1.25:1** — **the same two tones the filled span and the rail already were**, so the chart introduces no colour the component did not have. `1.4.11` is not owed on the bars: the band is said by the two thumbs, by the filled span and by two printed values, three carriers before the picture. **`WCAG 1.1.1` is answered with a sentence, not with `aria-hidden`** — `role="img"` and *How services are priced: most cost ₴200 – ₴350*, which describes the **distribution** and never the selection. **`WCAG 2.5.3 Label in Name` is a real fix here, not a preference:** the thumbs now stand under printed captions reading **Minimum** and **Maximum**, so `Lowest price` / `Highest price` became **`Minimum price`** / **`Maximum price`**; an accessible name that does not contain the visible word is exactly what the criterion is about. **A `<legend>` WAS BUILT FIRST AND MEASURED OUT, WHICH IS THE OPPOSITE OF THIS PROJECT'S USUAL ANSWER** — native needs no ARIA, and it is how the slider itself was argued. A rendered legend is placed in the fieldset's **block-start border region, outside the padding box**: measured in Chrome the title sat flush at the card's 0,0 and took the whole 16px inset down with it, bars and value fields to the card edge. The title is a `<p>` and `aria-labelledby` points at it, so the group's name is **the line the eye reads** rather than a duplicate string in an attribute — a better patch than the `aria-label="Time"` / `"Price"` this same panel already spends twice. **ONE MORE FINDING, AND IT WAS COSTING THE CARD ITS INSET SILENTLY:** the base sheet's `.dr fieldset{ padding: 0 }` is **0-1-1** and out-specifies any bare component class, so `.dr-range` is selected as **`.dr .dr-range`** (0-2-0). Any future component that *is* a `<fieldset>` meets the same wall. **Tokens: one new, one derived.** `--sz-hist` **48px** — not `--sz-mark` 52 and not `--h-groups` 48 wearing another hat, both of which name a box that is tapped or read, where this is the height of a picture. `--hist-seat` = `--sz-rail / 2 − --h-control / 2` = **−20px**, which seats the 44px slider lane so the rail's top edge meets the bars' baseline; measured difference **0.00px**, and derived so moving either source value cannot leave the picture floating above its line. **GREEN, RE-COUNTED ON THE FRAME AND UNCHANGED: 15,580px² = 5.12%** — `Apply filters` **15,004** (**96.3%** of the green, inside §5's 81–100% band) plus the Time zone's one checked radio **576**. **The new zone contributes 0px², and it also removes the frame's only way of gaining a fourth green mark** — a checked price band used to light a third radio at 576px² — so the ceiling is approached from further away than before. The ~5% overage is pre-existing and is left as found, as rev 66 and rev 70 left it. **Two things reported and not swept.** The zone label above the card still reads `PRICE ▸ what you expect to pay` while the card's own title now reads *Price range* — a mild repetition where `Location` and `Time` have none, and hers to settle. And rev 66's open point is still open: `listings-filtered` draws a chip per applied filter and the price dimension still has no chip. **On the bench, declared and spent nowhere, kept rather than deleted:** `.dr-range__out` (the one-line `₴0 – ₴1,000+` readout) and `.dr-range__ends` (the pair of end captions), both replaced by `.dr-range__cap` + `.dr-range__val`, and the `.dr-picks li + li .dr-range::before` hairline, which belonged to the band's old life as an `<li>`. **One documentation figure corrected by measurement:** `kit.css` said `--btn2` on `--card` was **1.31:1**; recomputed this pass it is **1.25:1**, which is what rev 66 recorded. Reported in place, the old number left standing rather than quietly restamped.

- **2026-08-16 (rev 124)** — **`account-edit`'s Personal group takes the card back: rev 98's strip gets an opt-out, not a reversal.** The designer on the built screen: *«зроби бекграунд для групи імя телефон та адреса щоб вже тоді все в картках було»*. **THE STANDARDS FIRST, AND THEY DO NOT PICK A SIDE.** `HIG · Text fields` ships **two** form styles and both conform — the **plain list** (Notes, Mail: rows on the page, a hairline between, no box) and the **grouped inset list** (Settings: a card, rows, separators indented from the card edge). rev 98 chose the plain list for input groups product-wide, and rev 103 took the same anatomy across the grayscale layer. **This screen is the product's Settings**: it configures a stored account, which is the exact case iOS draws as a stack of grouped inset cards, and the other three blocks on it are already cards — Preferences since rev 122, both upload zones since rev 60. So the Personal group was the one bare block among four, standing at a different left edge from the two rows directly beneath it. **NOTHING HERE PUTS A BORDER BACK ON A FIELD, WHICH IS WHAT REV 98 WAS ACTUALLY ABOUT.** Her rev 98 sentence was *«мені не подобається обводка в полях вводу»*, and that pass's finding was that the input had **no border** — what read as обводка was the panel. The input still computes **`border: 0px none`**, verified this pass, on the card as off it. What changes is the ground under the group, not the edge around the control. **THE MODIFIER IS OPT-IN, SO REV 98 STANDS EVERYWHERE IT WAS SET.** `.dr-rows--card` narrows the three strip rules with `:not(.dr-rows--card)` and is spent on **one group in the product**. Swept before and after: the six pages that **compose** rather than configure — `order-setup`, `order-setup-error`, `contact-support`, `rate`, `report-issue` and the `_field-options` spec — carry **zero** `.dr-rows--card`, so all seven of rev 98's input groups but this one render byte-identical. **MEASURED BY TOGGLING THE CLASS LIVE ON THE BUILT PAGE, 375 × 812, annotations off, before → after.** The group **does not move and does not resize**: **326.2 × 148 at the same x 288.8, y 168.8**. `background` `rgba(0,0,0,0)` → `--card` **`rgb(236,233,228)`**; `border-radius` **0 → 16** (`--r-card`, the ramp's card rung); the field takes its own 16 back, so the label goes **288.8 → 304.8** — **32** from the frame edge, level with *Payment method* and *Language* in the Preferences card below it; the separator indents **left 0 → 16**, which is the grouped list's own inset. Rows hold **44 · 44 · 44** (`HIG · 44pt target` ✓, `WCAG 2.5.8` ✓), `.dr-main` scrolls **642** in a **578** window before and after, and the frame overflows by **0**. **All four blocks now share one box** — **326.2** wide at x **288.8**: Personal **148**, Preferences **104**, upload **161**, upload **161**. **CONTRAST IS RECOMPUTED ON THE NEW GROUND RATHER THAN CARRIED FORWARD.** Label `--slate` `#5A5751` **6.62:1 on `--page` → 5.95:1 on `--card`**; typed value `--ink` `#1A1A1A` **15.99:1 → 14.37:1**. `WCAG 1.4.3` floors 15px/400 at **4.5:1**, so the weaker of the two clears by **1.45** and the product keeps its zero text-contrast failures. **The one value that gets weaker is reported, not absorbed:** the hairline `--line` `#D9D5CE` goes **1.34:1 on the page → 1.21:1 on the card**. Both sit under `WCAG 1.4.11`'s 3:1 and are permitted there on the same reading this kit has always used — a list separator is decoration and never the carrier of structure, the grouping stays programmatic in the DOM (`WCAG 1.3.1` ✓) — and the card edge now says the group boundary the hairline used to say alone, so what the eye gets is more, not less. **GREEN IS UNTOUCHED BY CONSTRUCTION and is therefore not re-counted:** no green element was added, moved or resized, `Save changes` is still the one green control at **15,004px² · 4.93%** of the frame, and `--card` is the surface tone the screen already spends three times. **DOM order, tab order, every string and every type step are unchanged** — `microcopy.md` does not move, and the pass is geometry and ground only. **ONE THING IS LEFT OPEN AND IT IS HERS.** Whether the six compose pages follow this group onto the card is **not** decided here: her sentence named this group, and rev 103 took the plain list across the whole layer on purpose, so extending it would be reversing a product-wide call rather than applying a local one. It is one class per page if she wants it. The alternative reading — that the whole product goes back to grouped inset lists and rev 98 is retired — is recorded here so it is not re-proposed later as a new idea. | `ui/kit.css`, `wireframes/account-edit.html`, `DESIGN.md`, `ui/inventory.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 124

- **2026-08-16 (rev 125)** — **The filter panel's other two zones become titled cards: three cards of one kind, and each one says its own name in the product.** The designer on the built `listings-filters`: *«добав заголовок і підзаголовок у два фільта вище Location & available date»*. **WHAT WAS THERE, AND WHY IT WAS A GAP AND NOT A PREFERENCE.** rev 123 gave the **Price** zone a title and a subtitle inside its card. **Location** and the time zone had neither: the only word standing over either was `.zlabel`, and `.zlabel` is the **wireframe's** structure annotation — mono, uppercase, drawn with the arrow-hint beside it, and hidden by the shell's annotation toggle, **which is how she reads the page**. So with annotations off the panel showed **two unnamed cards under one named one**, and the same reading that created `.dr-sect` for `account-edit`'s Documents zone on 2026-08-15 applies here — with one difference that matters: **that** heading stood *above* the cards and came off the next day on her word (rev 120), and this one sits *inside* the card, which is the shape she has kept every time it was built (both upload cards, the range card). **THE STANDARDS FIRST, AND THEY DO NOT FAIL EITHER WAY.** Nothing was broken before this pass: `WCAG 2.4.6 Headings and Labels` governs headings that exist and does not require one; `2.4.10 Section Headings` does ask for them and is **AAA**, and the project's target is **AA** (`CLAUDE.md`), so what the titles buy is a preference met, not a failure closed. `HIG · inset grouped lists` puts a quiet header above each group and is answered either way. What the pass *does* close is a real `4.1.2` weakness: the two zones were named by `aria-label="Location"` / `aria-label="Time"` — strings a screen reader hears and the eye never sees, free to drift from the page without anything showing it. **THE NAME IS NOW THE LINE THE EYE READS**: `aria-labelledby` → the title, `aria-describedby` → the subtitle, on the Location group and on the Time fieldset (`WCAG 1.3.1` ✓, `2.5.3` ✓, `4.1.2` ✓) — the third and fourth time this panel spends the patch rev 119 chose over a `<legend>`, and for rev 119's measured reason: a rendered legend sits in the fieldset's **block-start border region, outside the padding box**, and takes the card's whole 16px inset with it. The titles stay `<p>` and not `<h2>` for the same AA/AAA reading above; one attribute per card changes it and it is hers. **THE COMPONENT IS THE PRICE CARD'S OWN SHAPE, NOT A SECOND ONE.** `.dr-zone` is `.dr .dr-range` with the range taken out — `--card`, `--r-card` 16, a 16 inset, a 17/600 title, a 13/400 subtitle 4 under it, 20 to the content — and the two titles and the two subtitles are **one declaration each**, listed with `.dr-range__title` / `.dr-range__sub`, so a repeat of the component cannot drift from the card that already had it. It answers at **`.dr .dr-zone`** (0-2-0) for the wall rev 123 found: `.dr fieldset{ padding: 0 }` is 0-1-1, and the Time zone *is* a fieldset. `.dr-range` keeps its own class names on the one page that has it — renaming a built zone is not what was asked for, and one declaration already holds the values. **THE CARD BOUNDARY MOVED AND THE LISTS DID NOT — VERIFIED AFTER, NOT INTENDED BEFORE.** Location's four rows stand at **x 289 · 341 wide · 44 high**, labels at **305**, separator **305 → 630**; the four Time options at **x 305 · 309 · 44**, labels at **341**, separator **341 → 614**. Every one of those numbers is what it was before the pass. `.dr-picks` needed nothing but to give up its ground and its inset — its rows were inset 16 by the list and are inset 16 by the card. `.dr-rows` bleeds **−16** either side, because its rows carry their own 16 and its separator is drawn from that inset to the **card's** trailing edge (`HIG · Lists and tables`); without the bleed the line would have stopped 16px short of the edge it is drawn to. **WHAT IT COSTS, MEASURED WITH ANNOTATIONS OFF, WHICH IS HER VIEW — AND IT IS THE ONE THING IN THIS PASS THAT IS NOT FREE.** Each card grows **+66** for the two lines and the 20 gap, and Location **+16** more for taking the card's 16 inset where `.dr-rows` had 8: **192 → 273.99** and **208 → 273.99**, panel scroll **719 → 867** in a **577** window. The **Price** card's top moves **448 → 596**: it used to start above the fold with *Price range* and its subtitle readable without a scroll, and now **the whole zone is below the fold, by 19px**. Named rather than absorbed. The only lever that does not take back what she asked for is the two gaps — `--sp-group` 20 → `--sp-snug` 12 returns **16px** of the 148 and breaks step with the Price card's own rhythm; the other two levers are dropping a subtitle or dropping a title, which is the feedback undone. **Hers to say, and nothing is pre-decided.** **`Date & time`, NOT `Time`, and the divergence from the `.zlabel` is deliberate — the same divergence `Price` ▸ `Price range` already carries.** The zone sets a **day** and a time within it (*Available now · Today · This week · Pick a date & time…*), and *Time* alone reads as a time of day; it is also the wording the product already writes on the option's own label and on the drawer's title, so nothing is invented for it. Her own word for the filter was *available date*. One string in one file if she wants the annotation's single word instead. **THE TWO SUBTITLES ARE ONE SENTENCE SHAPE:** *Where you need the service* / *When you need the service* — `voice.md` **P1** (the outcome, never the drone) and **P4** (*service* is the product's own word, on the Home tiles, on `order-setup`, and in *Service price, all fees included* one card below). **GREEN IS UNTOUCHED AND WAS RE-COUNTED RATHER THAN ASSUMED: 15,580px² = 5.12%** of the frame — `Apply filters` **15,004** (96.3% of the green) plus the one checked radio **576**. The zones contribute **0px²**. **CONTRAST INTRODUCES NO NEW PAIR:** titles `--ink` on `--card` **14.37:1**, subtitles `--slate` on `--card` **5.95:1** — `WCAG 1.4.3` floors **both** at **4.5:1** — 17px/600 is *not* large text, which the criterion sets at 24px, or 18.66px bold — cleared by **9.87** and **1.45**. **ONE DOCUMENT WAS WRONG AND IS CORRECTED AGAINST THE FRAME:** `microcopy.md` had `Price range` recorded as a `<legend>`; the built page has a `<p>` named by `aria-labelledby`, which `kit.css` has said since rev 119. The frame is the source. | `ui/kit.css`, `wireframes/listings-filters.html`, `microcopy.md`, `wireframes/_screens.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `ui/inventory.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 125

- **2026-08-16 (rev 126)** — **The category row on `listings` starts working: four chips that were drawn and inert now filter the cards under them, and the screen at rest does not move by a pixel.** The designer on the built `listings`: *«зроби клікабельні чіпси ну і відповідно картки під ними по категоріях»*. **WHAT WAS THERE.** `<nav class="dr-groups" aria-label="Service categories">` with four `<button>`s and `aria-current="true"` on *All* — real buttons in the tab order, painted by `kit.css`, wired to nothing. A control that looks like a control and answers to no press is the one accessibility failure that measures clean: every ratio, every target and every name passed, and the tap did nothing. **THE STANDARDS FIRST, AND BOTH READINGS OF THE PATTERN CONFORM — SO THIS IS AN ANSWER, NOT A GAP.** A set of one-of-N controls that swaps the content below it can be built two ways. `WAI-ARIA APG · Tabs` makes the row a `role="tablist"` with `aria-selected`, one tab stop and arrow keys, and the list a `tabpanel`; the **filter-chip** reading keeps the buttons, keeps four tab stops and carries the state on `aria-current`. `WCAG 2.1.1 Keyboard` is met by both — verified on the build, `Enter` and `Space` each pick and `Tab` walks the row. `WCAG 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value` is met by both — `aria-current` is ARIA 1.2's own property for *the current item within a set*. **The filter-chip reading was built**, for two reasons that are the project's and not the spec's: `kit.css` has painted `[aria-current="true"]` on `.dr-grouptab` since the row was drawn, and `kit.js`'s picker already moves that exact attribute to land a pick — so the filter says *this one* the way the product already says it, and invents no third convention. The tabs reading is one attribute and a keydown handler away and is hers if she wants the arrow keys. **THE FRAME AT REST IS UNCHANGED — MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED.** *All* already carried `aria-current`, so the resting screen is what it was: chip heights **32** with the `::after` bleed at **−6 / −6** either side for a **44** hit area (`HIG · 44pt target` ✓, and it was already there), radius **999** the pill rung, the selected chip `--ink` on `--green-wash` at **14.75:1** (`WCAG 1.4.3` floor **4.5:1** for 13px — cleared by **10.25**), the unselected `--slate` on `--page` at **6.62:1**. **No new pair, no new token, no new component, no new string.** **GREEN IS UNTOUCHED AT THE COUNT THAT MATTERS: the filter adds 0px² of solid green.** The state carrier is `--green-wash`, the tint, which `CLAUDE.md` excludes from the ≤5% budget at **1.085:1** against the page — measured here at **1.08:1**. The widest wash the row can show is *Aerial photo & video* at **155.3 × 32 = 4,970px², 1.63%** of the 375 × 812 frame, and it replaces the wash *All* was already wearing. **`hidden` ON THE `<li>`, NOT A CLASS ON THE CARD.** A filtered-out service leaves the layout and the accessibility tree together, so the screen reader counts what the eye counts (`WCAG 1.3.1`) and its card link is not a focus stop pointing at a service the screen no longer offers (`WCAG 2.4.3`). Measured across the four picks: focus stops in the list **3 → 1 → 1 → 1**, exactly one chip holds `aria-current` in every one of them. **NOTHING IS ANNOUNCED, AND THAT IS A DECISION.** The counter row left all five listing states on her own word — *«remove this and sorting — I have filters instead»* — so a `role="status"` here would put `3 services` back on the page through the side door. **Nothing fails at AA for it:** `WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages` governs a status message a page *presents*, and this page presents none; the change is spoken by the chip the user just pressed, which is where the focus is. Whether the filter should say *1 service* out loud is hers, and it sits beside the same open question `listings-loading` was left with on 2026-08-16. **THE ARITHMETIC OF THE CATALOGUE, REPORTED AND NOT SOLVED:** `sitemap.md` CE-2 holds **three** service types and a service belongs to exactly one, so *Delivery*, *Aerial photo & video* and *Inspection* each resolve to **one card** and *All* to three. The chips name the same three things the cards do, and a tap hides two rows. That is the catalogue being what it is, not the filter being wrong — and the only way it changes is more services, which is content and hers to write into `sitemap.md` first. **SCOPE HELD TO THE PAGE SHE POINTED AT.** `-filtered`, `-empty`, `-error` and `-loading` carry the same chip row and it is still inert on all four; they are separate pages, the call was made on the base, and carrying it over uninvited is not what the standing rule allows. **The page's one script**, next to `listings-filters`' two, and for the same stated reason: `ui/kit.js` is the picker drawer and says so at the top. | `wireframes/listings.html`, `wireframes/_screens.md`, `ui/inventory.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 126

- **2026-08-16 (rev 127)** — **The titled card goes to every group of entered data in the product, and the sweep that decided where it stops is the larger half of the pass.** The designer, straight after the filter panel took its two titles: *«якщо в проекті ще є аналогічні картки добав до них так само заголовок і підзаголовок але не у картках замовлень та оператора а там де є необхідність вводити дані які посортовані по категоріях»*. **THE RULE SHE GAVE IS NOT «HAS A CARD», IT IS «HOLDS DATA THE USER ENTERS, SORTED INTO CATEGORIES» — so the product was swept by computed style before a line was written, and a group counted only where its background computes `--card`.** What came back is short and that is the finding: **`account-edit` · Personal** (three typed rows) and **· Preferences** (two picker rows), and **`payment` · Payment method** (three options). **THE FOUR SCREENS THAT LOOK LIKE CANDIDATES AND ARE NOT.** `order-setup`, `report-issue`, `contact-support` and `rate` all collect typed data under a zone label, and every one of their groups computes **`rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)`** — they are **plain lists**, not cards, because **rev 98** made every input group one on her own word (*«мені не подобається обводка в полях вводу… зроби як у HIG»*) and **rev 103** carried it across the grayscale layer. What is added now is a title **inside** a card, which is the shape she has kept every single time it was built: both upload cards, the range card, and now these three. The rev 120 entry closed by saying that if the pair should be named again *it comes back as a heading above the pair … and it is decided here first* — that heading is still not back, and the two upload cards are untouched. **THE COPY CARRIES THE ONE DISTINCTION THE SCREENS CANNOT DRAW.** `account-edit`'s card reads **Payment & language ▸ *How you pay and the language you see*** and is the **saved default**; `payment`'s reads **Payment method ▸ *How you pay for this order*** and is **this order's** choice. The words *for this order* are the only thing on either screen that says which is which. `Personal details ▸ What the operator needs to reach you` says what its three rows are **for** rather than listing them again — `voice.md` **P1**, the outcome and not the equipment. The cards contribute **0px²**. **ACCESSIBILITY GAINS RATHER THAN HOLDS.** Every card is now named by its drawn title (`aria-labelledby`) and described by its subtitle (`aria-describedby`), which retires `aria-label="Payment method"` on `payment` and — the real repair — gives `account-edit`'s two groups **the programmatic name they never had**: they were bare `<div>`s, so the grouping a person could see was invisible to a screen reader (`WCAG 1.3.1`), and drawing a title over them without the role would have widened that gap rather than closed it (`4.1.2`, `2.5.3`). **`.dr-zone` LEAVES §3 One-off after one day** — three screens clears `ui/inventory.md`'s own 2+ rule — and takes a kit row in §1.3, which goes **13 → 14**. | `microcopy.md`, `wireframes/_screens.md`, `wireframes/account-edit.html`, `wireframes/payment.html`, `ui/inventory.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 127

- **2026-08-16 (rev 128)** — **The profile photo becomes a control, and the screen behind it gets built.** The designer on `account`: *«добав іконку едіт карандаш на аватарку і добав ще один wireframe де можна загрузити аватарку зроби його по HIG вимогам»*. **THE TARGET IS THE PICTURE, NOT THE BADGE.** A 20pt disc is half of `HIG · 44pt`, so the `<a>` wraps the photo and the badge together and measures **56 × 56** — 127% of the floor and 2.3× `WCAG 2.5.8`, cleared without a hit-area hack; the badge is `aria-hidden` and the link carries the name. **THE BADGE SPENDS NO NEW MATERIAL.** `--btn2` ground with an `--ink` glyph, **11.54:1** — the pair the sibling `.dr-btn--icon` already wears on that card, so two edit affordances read as one thing. The disc is **1.25:1** on the card and is not the identifier; the pencil is, exactly as `.dr-upload`'s 1.08:1 well defers to its 32px mark. Base only: an empty avatar is the base (the initials fallback), and no `flows.md` node produces an error or a loading state for it. Built from components that already exist — the preview card, two `.dr-upload--card` zones (`HIG · Action sheets` names both sources by name: *Take a photo*, *Choose from library*), and the stacked action bar rev 120 gave every bottom edge. **MEASURED ON THE BUILD, ANNOTATIONS OFF.** Radii **12 · 16 · 999**, all inside the closed ramp. Shadows: **one**, `--sh-sm` under *Save photo*. Green **15,004px² · 4.93%** of the frame and the primary is **100%** of it — inside the 4.43–5.09 band the painted set already holds. Contrast, every pair: upload titles **14.37:1**, notes **5.95:1**, *Remove photo* **6.83:1**, its consequence line **6.62:1**, *Save photo* **9.46:1**, *Cancel* **11.54:1** — `WCAG 1.4.3` ✓ with nothing near the floor. - **2026-08-16 (rev 129)** — **A finished order card stops showing the drone and starts showing the result.** The designer, on `order-setup` but about the history: *«на сторінках де замовник переглядає замовлення які раніше були для нього зроблені щоб він бачив як зробили, бо раптом він замовляв багато послуг і вже не пам'ятає — наприклад кришу обстежував тричі, то йому потрібно наочно бачити щоб згадати що там йому було зроблено»*. **THE ARGUMENT IS RECALL, AND THE OLD WELL WAS CARRYING THE ONE FACT THAT CANNOT CARRY IT.** rev 33 put the machine in the well because *the payload names the service* — a lime cargo box for delivery, a gimbal camera for aerial, an empty airframe for inspection — and that is still true of a **running** order. It fails the moment a client has ordered the same service more than once: three roof inspections draw `drone-flight-clean` three times, so the picture is identical exactly where the client needs a difference. The service name and the date already say *what* and *when*; only the photograph says *which one*. **WHAT WAS BUILT.** `.dr-well--shot` — the third case in `kit.css` §MEDIA after `--photo` (a surface that sizes itself) and `--map`. It keeps the well's **96 × 72** box that the card lays out and changes treatment alone: inset **0**, `object-fit: cover`, `--media` as the pre-load ground, clipped at `--r-media` **13**. Measured on the build, both filled wells: **96.0 × 72.0**, radius **13px**, `cover` — bit-identical geometry to the two cards that still carry a cutout, so nothing in the card moved. **TWO CARDS TOOK IT, TWO DID NOT, FOR TWO DIFFERENT REASONS — AND ONLY ONE OF THEM IS A DECISION.** The 28 Jun delivery takes `scenes/thumbs/delivered-at-door.jpg` and the 2 Jun inspection takes `scenes/thumbs/inspection-roof-cracked-tiles.jpg` — **the exact frame that opens that order's report**, so the card and `inspection-report` show the same picture and the client lands on what they tapped. The **live** card keeps `drone-08`: there is no result yet, and *what is flying* is the honest answer while it flies. The **aerial** card keeps `drone-flight-camera` only because the repo holds no picture of an aerial deliverable — `visuals/gaps.md` **B3 #17**, still open. That one is a gap, not a rule, and it is said here so the mixed list is not later read as a design choice. **`WCAG 1.4.11` RE-MEASURED ON THE STATUS MARK, BECAUSE THE PHOTOGRAPH IS THE ONE THING THAT COULD HAVE BROKEN IT.** The mark is a 20px disc riding the well's top-right corner with a 2px `--card` ring. **The state is carried by the glyph, not by the disc:** the check is `--ink` on the disc's own opaque `--btn2` at **11.54:1**, and the picture never touches it — the criterion is met with **8.54** to spare on a 3:1 floor. The ring, sampled every 15° against the photograph behind it, reads **1.50 : 1 at worst and 12.25 : 1 at best**; so it is a separator that sometimes disappears into a bright pixel, not the thing that says *delivered*. That is the same reading rev 128 already stood on, where a 1.25:1 badge defers to its pencil. **`--sh-onphoto` WAS CONSIDERED AND DELIBERATELY NOT SPENT.** §Shadows holds it *for the first chip that actually floats over a photograph*, and this is literally that chip — but a soft shadow improves the seam perceptually and still guarantees no ratio, and spending the product's second shadow to fix a boundary that is not the state indicator would trade the flat rule for nothing measurable. It stays held. **GREEN IS UNTOUCHED**: the two wells that changed carry a **settled check** (`--btn2`, no green); the only solid green on the screen is the live card's ping and the `View details` primary, exactly as counted at **4.43%**. **ONE BUDGET IS BREACHED AND IT IS REPORTED RATHER THAN SMOOTHED.** Both photographs are the `thumbs/` copies cut for `delivery`'s and `inspection-report`'s **341px** boxes — 652 × 522 and 652 × 489 — so a **96px** well draws them at **6.79×** against §6.2's *serve ≈2× the CSS box*. Nothing renders wrong; the cost is 199 KB fetched for two 96px thumbnails. The fix is a **192 × 144** card cut of each, which adds files to `assets/` and is hers to authorise. **`drones/thumbs/drone-flight-clean.png` NOW DRAWS NOWHERE** — its `wide/` cut is still `welcome`'s third slide, and the file is kept, not deleted. | `wireframes/order-history.html`, `ui/kit.css`, `ui/inventory.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `visuals/gaps.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 129

- **2026-08-16 (rev 130)** — **Tapping a finished order opened a form that could still be edited; it now opens the order's record. `order-details.html` is built.** The designer on the built `order-history`: *«тут помилка — коли я відкриваю замовлення що вже було виконане для мене, то я бачу самері звідки куди, вага яка була, пруф фото що мені надіслав оператор; поля вводу коментів лишати у мене немає вже і вагу я теж вибрати вже не можу, і кнопки мають бути в ботом барі — замовити ще раз чи видалити. Або цього wireframe немає, або ти невірно залінкував»*. **IT WAS THE SECOND, AND ON ALL THREE CARDS.** Every finished card carried `href="order-setup.html"` — the live setup form — so a client opening an order that closed in June was handed a parcel-size picker, an empty notes field and *Continue to review*. The screen she describes did not exist anywhere: `sitemap.md`, `flows.md` and `_screens.md` C4 all ran history → setup with nothing between them. **A SUB-VIEW, NOT A SITEMAP SCREEN — the `account-photo` precedent from rev 128 applied deliberately.** `sitemap.md` §6.1 still lists one *Order history*; this is its second file, no three-letter code is minted, and neither traceability matrix gains a column. **THE 2-TAP TARGET IS NOT TOUCHED, AND THAT WAS CHECKED BEFORE THE SCREEN WAS DRAWN.** `sitemap.md` §7.3 counts *Book again* at 2 taps **from a shortcut**, and `flows.md` already prints a red *long way* badge over the Home → history → setup branch, calling it the no-shortcut fallback. Inserting a record screen lengthens the fallback by one and leaves the target where it was. **STATE MATRIX: BASE ONLY, AND THE REASON IS WRITTEN INTO `_screens.md` RATHER THAN LEFT TO INFERENCE.** A finished order cannot be empty, has nothing to load that the list did not already load, and no `flows.md` node produces an error on it. **BUILT ENTIRELY FROM COMPONENTS THAT EXIST** — `.dr-rows` for the summary, `.dr-rows--money` for the cost, `delivery.html`'s own 341 × 180 `.dr-well--photo`, and the stacked action bar. **No new class, no new token, no new rung.** **MEASURED ON THE BUILD, ANNOTATIONS OFF.** **Zero input controls** — the count is 0 `input` / `textarea` / `select` / `contenteditable` inside the frame, which is the whole of her complaint answered as a number. Contrast, every pair: topbar title **15.99:1**, row keys **5.95:1**, row values and *Paid* **14.37:1**, *Book again* **9.46:1**, *Delete order* **6.83:1** — `WCAG 1.4.3` floor 4.5:1 for all of them (15px/600 is not large text), cleared by **1.45** at the tightest. Targets: back **44 × 44**, both buttons **341 × 44** — `HIG · 44pt` met exactly, none short. Radii **12 · 13 · 16**, three rungs of the closed ramp and nothing off it. **Shadows: one** — `--sh-sm` under the green primary; the second box-shadow the DOM reports belongs to the shell's own `EN` chip and is not product. **Green: 15,022px² = 4.93%** of the 375 × 812 frame, the primary being **100%** of it — inside the 4.43–5.09 band the painted set already holds. **THE PHOTOGRAPH IS THE SAME FILE AND THE SAME BOX AS `delivery.html`'s**, 341.4 × 180.0, which is the point: the proof does not get re-cropped for the archive. **WHAT SEPARATES THIS SCREEN FROM `delivery.html`, stated so the pair does not read as a duplicate:** `delivery` is the order at the moment it lands — a milestone mark, *Your parcel arrived at 10:07*, ending in *Rate this order*. This is the same order months later, ending in *Book again*. One is an event, the other is a record; they share the photograph and nothing else. **TWO THINGS REPORTED AND NOT BUILT.** (1) **`Delete order` deletes with no confirmation** — it is a link straight back to the list, and the trash control on the card behind it has always behaved the same way; `HIG · Alerts` asks a destructive, unrecoverable action to confirm, and closing it means a confirm sheet on both, which is a component decision and hers. (2) **The card's icon control is still named *View details*** while the card itself now opens the order — two controls one word apart, which is the ambiguity `WCAG 2.4.6` names and which the page's own annotation already describes differently (*open the delivery photo / report*). Renaming it is a string change and is left for her word. **ONE SEAM CARRIED OVER, NOT INTRODUCED:** all three finished cards open this one instance — the 28 Jun delivery — so tapping *Roof inspection* shows a parcel. The prototype is showing one pattern with one page; an inspection instance would carry the report cover in the well and a single *Location* row. | `wireframes/order-details.html` (new), `wireframes/order-history.html`, `wireframes/_screens.md`, `wireframes/_conventions.md`, `wireframes/_wf-shell.js`, `flows.md`, `microcopy.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 130

- **2026-08-16 (rev 131)** — **`Delete order` goes grey: the destructive control on `order-details` drops `--danger-ink` for the secondary rung.** The designer, on the button as built: *«зроби кнопку секондарі сірою»*. **THE STANDARDS PULL APART HERE, AND BOTH READINGS ARE STATED BEFORE THE CHANGE RATHER THAN AFTER.** `HIG · Alerts / Action sheets` asks a destructive choice to *read* as destructive — red is how iOS makes a thumb pause — and rev 128 spent `.dr-btn--danger` on `Remove photo` for exactly that reason. `WCAG` names no colour for danger at all: `1.4.1 Use of Colour` asks the opposite, that colour never be the **only** carrier of meaning. **Her reading conforms, and it conforms harder than the red did.** With the colour gone, the verb `Delete` is the only carrier there is, so `1.4.1` is not merely held but is unable to fail. **MEASURED, BEFORE AND AFTER.** Red: `--danger-ink` #9A3115 as text, **6.83:1**. Grey: `--ink` on `--btn2`, **11.54:1** — a **+4.71** gain, and the same pair `Cancel` already wears in `account-photo`'s action bar, so no new pair enters the product. Box **341 × 44** (`HIG · 44pt` ✓), radius **12**, no shadow. The button's fill sits **1.39:1** against the action bar, which is the secondary rung's own long-standing property across the product and not something this change introduced; the control is identified by its label, which reads at 11.54:1. **GREEN IS UNMOVED at 15,022px² · 4.93%**, the primary still 100% of it, and the frame still carries exactly one shadow. **WHAT IS GIVEN UP, SAID PLAINLY:** the warning at a glance. A grey `Delete order` sitting directly under a green `Book again` is two buttons of equal visual weight, one of which is unrecoverable, and the screen has no confirm sheet behind it (still open from rev 130). Whether the pause comes back as a confirm rather than as a colour is the live question, and it is hers. **THE PRODUCT NOW STYLES ITS TWO DESTRUCTIVE ACTIONS DIFFERENTLY** — `Remove photo` on `account-photo` is still red. Reported, not silently aligned: `account-photo` is a screen she settled one revision ago and was not what she pointed at. | `wireframes/order-details.html`, `microcopy.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 131
- **2026-08-16 (rev 132)** — **The finished order states what it cost, not how it was priced: the breakdown comes off `order-details`.** The designer on the built record: *«на цих wireframes що показують деталі вже виконаного замовлення лише остаточна сума без розшифровки»*. `Base fare ₴90` · `Distance · 7.4 km ₴70` · `Service fee ₴20` are removed; **`Paid ₴180` stays**. **THE `.MD` SOURCES ALREADY SAID THIS, WHICH IS WHY NO STRING HAD TO BE RETIRED.** `microcopy.md`'s master table has listed exactly **one** string in this zone since the screen was built at rev 130 — `Paid` — and `ui/inventory.md` has recorded `.dr-rows--money` as *"spent on `order-review`'s breakdown and its loading state alone"* since rev 97. The three line items lived in the HTML and in neither document; this change makes the build agree with its sources rather than the other way round. **WHERE THE BREAKDOWN BELONGS, WRITTEN DOWN SO IT IS NOT RE-ADDED:** `voice.md` **P3** states the price as a fact *before the tap*, and `order-review` is that tap — its four rows are what the client agrees to be charged. Afterwards the money has already moved: a record answers *how much*, not *how it was computed*. **MEASURED ON THE BUILD, ANNOTATIONS OFF, 375 × 812.** Money card **213.4 → 70.0** (one row **54**, plus the panel's own 8/8 inset). `main` **577.6**, unchanged. **AND THE RECORD STOPS SCROLLING — `scrollHeight` 712 → 578, against `clientHeight` 578.** The 134px it was over by was the breakdown, exactly; summary, photograph, sum and both buttons now stand inside the frame at once, which is what a record is for. Horizontal overflow **0**. **NOTHING ELSE MOVES:** green **15,022px² · 4.93%** with the primary still **100%** of it, one shadow, **zero** input controls, radii unchanged. `Paid` keeps its pair — key and value both `--ink` on `--card`, **14.37:1** (`WCAG 1.4.3` floor 4.5:1 ✓; 15px/600 is not large text) — and the figure keeps the scale's money step, **20/700 tabular**. The row's hairline disappears because `.dr-rows__row:last-child` drops it; no rule changed. **ONE THING THE CHANGE CREATES, AND IT IS HERS TO SETTLE — ASKED, NOT TAKEN:** the lone figure still sits in the **trailing money column**, value right edge **342.2** at the card's inner edge, while all five Summary values above it read from the **left axis at 182.8** (rev 97: `--sz-rowkey` 140, value 159). `.dr-rows--money` exists for one stated reason — *tabular numerals only line up on the last digit if the column is right-aligned* — and with one row there is no column to line up. Both alignments conform (`HIG · Lists and tables` ships the trailing value, `HIG · Text fields` the leading one; `WCAG 1.4.3` / `1.3.1` untouched either way), so it goes to her the way rev 97 itself went: numbers first, taste call second. **`ui/inventory.md`'s count is left standing until that answer** — its sentence names `.dr-rows--money` as `order-review`'s alone, which her call either makes true again or corrects to name this row. | `wireframes/order-details.html`, `microcopy.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 132

- **2026-08-16 (rev 129)** — **Pay stops being a link to a screen and becomes a drawer, and the action bar joins the picker as something that can open one.** The designer on `order-review`: *«по кліку на pay має відкриватись дровер а не сторінка і кнопки з іконкою чорного кольору apple pay та google pay або можливість обрати картку як на цьому зразку лиш усі кнопки залиті праймері»* — the sample being Figma `YlGWlsWWjKSCxhONMzGG2F` node `95:10`, an eSIM checkout whose bottom edge is a **black** Apple Pay button over a *Pay with card* text action. **THE COUNT RULE DECIDED IT AND DID NOT HAVE TO BEND.** Three ways to pay is ≤ 6, so the list rises from the bottom edge rather than pushing a screen — the same sentence that has governed every picker since rev 102 — and the drawer keeps the grabber and the named `Cancel` that `HIG · Action sheets` asks for. What is new is only *what may carry* `aria-controls`. **THE OPENER WIDENED BY EXACTLY ONE SHAPE, AND THE OBVIOUS WIDENING WAS THE WRONG ONE.** `ui/kit.js` answered to `.dr-field--action[aria-controls]`; it now also answers to `.dr-btn[aria-controls]`. The tempting move — the bare `[aria-haspopup="dialog"][aria-controls]` — was measured and rejected: `listings-filters`' date opener is a `.sr-only` **radio** carrying both of those attributes and served by its own page script, so the generic selector would have put two handlers on one control. `.dr-btn[aria-controls]` matched **0** elements across `wireframes/` before this pass. Focus also gained a fallback to the sheet's first control, because a pay option is a button and not a `.dr-picker__item` (`WCAG 2.4.3`). **ONE CSS RULE AND NO NEW BUTTON VARIANT.** `.dr-paylist` is a column at **8**, the action bar's own rhythm, against the sheet's **12** out to `Cancel` — siblings closer than strangers. A pay option is a plain `.dr-btn--primary.dr-btn--block`; its label is the brand mark, so it needs no class. The marks are centred inside their 40 × 20 box where the picker row's copies anchor left, and they are the rev 108 artwork reused, still approximations until handoff. **IT FORKS A FLOW NODE RATHER THAN DELETING ONE.** *Pay with card* pushes **Payment**, so that screen and its error / loading states stay on the main path; Apple Pay and Google Pay skip it — the method is already chosen — and land on **`payment-loading`**, *"Authorizing your payment…"*, which is where an express charge actually is. Nothing is orphaned. **MEASURED OPEN AT THE 375 FRAME.** Sheet **373 × 292.8**, `--r-panel` 22, `--sh-raised`. Three options **341 × 44** at `--r-btn` 12; `Cancel` **341 × 44** on `--btn2` **11.54:1**. Radii inside the sheet: **999 · 12**, both in the closed ramp. Ink: brand marks `#000` **11.42:1** on the green, DRON's own card glyph and words `--on-green` **9.46:1** — `WCAG 1.4.3` and `1.4.11` ✓ throughout, and logotypes are exempt from both anyway. Every control **≥ 44 × 44**. **THE MODAL CONTRACT WAS VERIFIED, NOT ASSERTED.** A real dispatched pointer open shows **no** focus ring (`:focus-visible` false) and a keyboard open does; five Tabs cycle the sheet's four stops and never leave it; it closes **three ways** — Esc, the scrim, `Cancel` — each clearing `aria-expanded` and handing focus back to the CTA; `.dr-main`, `.dr-topbar` and `.dr-actionbar` are all `inert` while it is up. **TWO DEPARTURES, BOTH HERS, BOTH REPORTED WITH THE NUMBER AND NEITHER SILENTLY FIXED.** (1) `HIG · Apple Pay Marks and Buttons` and Google Pay's brand guidelines require a button that **initiates** their payment to be black, white, or white-with-outline, and forbid a custom fill — these three initiate and are `--green`. Contrast is not what fails (`#000` on green is 11.42:1); the brand licence is, and a real integration would be rejected at review. Her own reference is Apple-conformant *because* its button is black. (2) §0's one-green rule — exactly one green control, ≤ ~5% of the frame — is exceeded **3×**: three block buttons are **45,012px² · 14.78%** (19.71% counting the CTA dimmed behind the scrim), and no one of them is the primary. **The conforming variant is a two-line change** — Apple and Google in their own black, `Pay with card` the single green — and it closes both at once, which is why it is offered rather than argued. **NOT SWEPT, AND STATED:** `order-review-loading`'s bottom edge is *Continue* → `order-review`, not a Pay button, so it has nothing to convert; `payment.html` keeps its own three methods for the card path and for anyone who opens it directly. | `wireframes/order-review.html`, `wireframes/_screens.md`, `wireframes/_conventions.md`, `ui/kit.js`, `ui/kit.css`, `ui/inventory.md`, `ui/inventory.html`, `microcopy.md`, `DESIGN.md`, `concept/concept.md` rev 129
